(!acc) return;\nvar items = Array.prototype.slice.call(acc.querySelectorAll('[data-haccordion-item]'));\nif (!items.length) return;\n// CSS-Länge (rem/px) → px. Wurzel-Font-Size für rem.\nfunction cssLenToPx(v) {\nv = (v || '').trim();\nvar n = parseFloat(v);\nif (isNaN(n)) return 0;\nif (v.indexOf('rem') !== -1) {\nvar root = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize) || 16;\nreturn n * root;\n}\nreturn n; // px (oder einheitenlos)\n}\n// Feste Inhaltsbreite EXAKT bestimmen (Accordion ist evtl. nicht 100vw):\n// open-w = innen − Spine-offen − (Anzahl−1) × Spine-zu. Spine-Breiten aus den\n// CSS-Vars gelesen (NICHT offsetWidth) → immun gegen laufende width-Animation.\n// Überschreibt die CSS-Fallback-Formel. Auf Mobile ignoriert (width:100%).\nfunction setOpenWidth() {\n// Nur im Horizontal-Modus messen (≥992px). Vertikal nutzt width:100%.\nif (window.matchMedia('(max-width: 991px)').matches) {\nacc.style.removeProperty('--haccordion-open-w');\nreturn;\n}\nvar cs = getComputedStyle(acc);\nvar openSpineW = cssLenToPx(cs.getPropertyValue('--haccordion-spine-open'));\nvar closedSpineW = cssLenToPx(cs.getPropertyValue('--haccordion-spine-closed'));\nif (!openSpineW) return;\nvar inner = acc.clientWidth - parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft || 0) - parseFloat(cs.paddingRight || 0);\nvar openW = Math.floor(inner - openSpineW - (items.length - 1) * closedSpineW);\nif (openW > 0) acc.style.setProperty('--haccordion-open-w', openW + 'px');\n}\nsetOpenWidth();\n// Resize-Guard: Transitions kurz aus (kein Breakpoint-/Mess-Jank),\n// nach Ruhe wieder an → nur Toggle-Klicks animieren.\nvar resizeTimer;\nwindow.addEventListener('resize', function () {\nacc.classList.add('is-resizing');\nsetOpenWidth();\nclearTimeout(resizeTimer);\nresizeTimer = setTimeout(function () { acc.classList.remove('is-resizing'); }, 120);\n});\nfunction open(item) {\nitems.forEach(function (it) {\nvar on = it === item;\nit.classList.toggle('is-open', on);\nvar sp = it.querySelector('.haccordion__spine');\nif (sp) sp.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(on));\n});\n}\n// Mobile: beim Öffnen den ANFANG des Accordions (erstes Item) smooth an\n// den oberen Rand holen (3rem Abstand via scroll-margin-top am Container),\n// NICHT das geöffnete Item – so bleiben die geschlossenen Items darüber\n// sichtbar. Container-Top ist stabil, daher keine Verzögerung nötig.\nvar mqMobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 991px)');\nfunction scrollOpenIntoView(item) {\nif (!mqMobile.matches) return;\nacc.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });\n}\n// Fokus auf den nächsten SICHTBAREN Spine (offenes Item überspringen)\nfunction focusSpine(item, dir) {\nvar i = items.indexOf(item);\nfor (var s = 0; s < items.length; s++) {\ni = (i + dir + items.length) % items.length;\nif (items[i].classList.contains('is-open')) continue; // offenes überspringen\nvar sp = items[i].querySelector('.haccordion__spine');\nif (sp) { sp.focus(); break; }\n}\n}\nitems.forEach(function (item) {\nvar spine = item.querySelector('.haccordion__spine');\nif (!spine) return;\nif (!spine.hasAttribute('tabindex')) spine.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');\nspine.addEventListener('click', function () { open(item); scrollOpenIntoView(item); });\nspine.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\nif (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\ne.preventDefault();\nopen(item);\nscrollOpenIntoView(item);\n// Spine klappt zu → Fokus in den offenen Inhalt (CTA)\nvar cta = item.querySelector('.haccordion__cta a, a[href]');\nif (cta) cta.focus();\n} else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') {\ne.preventDefault(); focusSpine(item, 1);\n} else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') {\ne.preventDefault(); focusSpine(item, -1);\n}\n});\n});\n}\nif (document.readyState !== 'loading') init();\nelse document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ('0'+n).slice(-2); }\nfunction iso(d){ return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate()); }\nfunction parseISO(s){ var p=s.split('-'); return new Date(+p[0],+p[1]-1,+p[2]); }\nfunction monthKey(d){ return d.getFullYear()+'-'+(d.getMonth()+1); }\nfunction initCalendar(root){\nvar calEl=root.querySelector('[data-gce-cal]'); if(!calEl||calEl.dataset.ready) return;\ncalEl.dataset.ready='1';\nvar DOW=JSON.parse(calEl.dataset.weekdays);\nvar MON=JSON.parse(calEl.dataset.months);\nvar EVENTS=JSON.parse(calEl.dataset.events).map(function(e){ return { s:parseISO(e.s), e:parseISO(e.e) }; });\nvar EDM={}; JSON.parse(calEl.dataset.edm).forEach(function(k){ EDM[k]=true; });\nvar today=parseISO(calEl.dataset.today);\nvar gridEl=calEl.querySelector('[data-gce-cal-grid]');\nvar titleEl=calEl.querySelector('[data-gce-cal-title]');\nvar prevBtn=calEl.querySelector('[data-gce-cal-prev]');\nvar nextBtn=calEl.querySelector('[data-gce-cal-next]');\nvar todayBtn=calEl.querySelector('[data-gce-cal-today]');\nvar listTitle=root.querySelector('[data-gce-list-title]');\nvar listEl=root.querySelector('[data-gce-list]');\nvar emptyEl=root.querySelector('[data-gce-list-empty]');\nvar edmWraps=root.querySelectorAll('[data-gce-edm-wrap]');\nvar scrollEl=root.querySelector('[data-gce-list-scroll]');\nvar moreWrap=root.querySelector('[data-gce-more-wrap]');\nvar moreBtn=root.querySelector('[data-gce-more]');\nvar MOBILE_LIMIT=3;\nvar minMonth=new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), 1);\n// Obergrenze = letzter Monat mit Events\nvar maxMonth=new Date(minMonth);\nEVENTS.forEach(function(ev){\nvar m=new Date(ev.e.getFullYear(), ev.e.getMonth(), 1);\nif(m>maxMonth) maxMonth=m;\n});\nvar view=new Date(minMonth);\nvar selected=null; // ISO-Tag oder null\nvar showAll=false; // mobiler „Mehr anzeigen\"-State\nfunction isMobile(){ return !window.matchMedia('(min-width:992px)').matches; }\nfunction overlapsDay(ev, d){ return ev.s<=d && ev.e>=d; }\nfunction overlapsMonth(ev, y, m){\nvar first=new Date(y,m,1), last=new Date(y,m+1,0);\nreturn ev.s<=last && ev.e>=first;\n}\nfunction countOn(d){ var n=0; EVENTS.forEach(function(ev){ if(overlapsDay(ev,d)) n++; }); return n; }\nfunction monthHasEvents(y,m){ return EVENTS.some(function(ev){ return overlapsMonth(ev,y,m); }); }\nfunction nearestMonthWithEvents(from, dir){\nvar d=new Date(from.getFullYear(), from.getMonth()+dir, 1);\nwhile(d>=minMonth && d<=maxMonth){\nif(monthHasEvents(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth())) return d;\nd=new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth()+dir, 1);\n}\nreturn null;\n}\nfunction monthTitle(d){ return (MON[d.getMonth()]+' '+d.getFullYear()).toUpperCase(); }\nfunction renderGrid(){\nvar y=view.getFullYear(), m=view.getMonth();\nvar startDow=(new Date(y,m,1).getDay()+6)%7; // Mo=0\nvar class=\"loc-overlay__header\">\n<span class=\"loc-overlay__ort text-size-small\">Zurich</span>\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__name\" id=\"loc-overlay-title-214\" data-no-translation=\"\">Privatclub HAUTE</div>\n<span class=\"loc-overlay__personen is-handwritten\">Up to 80 people</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__description text-size-regular\"><p>High above the rooftops of Zurich, this exclusive club restaurant combines a breathtaking view over the city, Lake Zurich and the Alps with a stylish, relaxed ambience. This unique setting creates the perfect backdrop for unforgettable private and business events. Events are generally open to all guests, provided a HAUTE member is present and hosts the event under their membership. From Monday to Friday, events for up to 50 guests can be held in the separate area. At weekends, the club restaurant is available exclusively for events with up to 80 guests.</p>\n</div>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__info-heading\">CAPACITY</div>\n<div class=\"info-grid\">\n<div class=\"info-item\" style=\"grid-column: span 2;\">\n<span class=\"info-item__text\">Restaurant during the week<br>\n50 people</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"info-item\" style=\"grid-column: span 2;\">\n<span class=\"info-item__text\">1/2 Weekday Terrace (Summer)<br>\n30 people</span>\n</div>\n<div Charles Hall</div>\n<span class=\"loc-overlay__personen is-handwritten\">Up to 150 people</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__description text-size-regular\"><p>At Villa St. Charles Hall in Meggen, even the foyer is impressive. The rooms — the Ritter, Wiener and Gobelin halls — offer a stylish backdrop for events of every kind. The beautiful park, which stretches all the way down to the lakeshore, also opens up countless possibilities for events held outdoors.</p>\n</div>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div 2000, the Schiffbau Production Centre has been part of the Schauspielhaus Zürich. The former shipyard hall, with its industrial charm, houses two performance spaces, rehearsal stages, as well as studios and workshops. This listed building combines history with modern infrastructure and offers an exclusive ambiance for events of all sizes in Zurich West.</p>\n</div>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div Baummuseum</div>\n<span class=\"loc-overlay__personen is-handwritten\">Up to 350 people</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__description text-size-regular\"><p>The event location at the Enea Tree Museum combines modern architecture with a dreamlike park landscape on Lake Zurich. The showroom, orangery, and tea house are unique spaces for corporate events, weddings, or cultural gatherings. The combination of nature, design, and flexible indoor and outdoor areas creates an exclusive and inspiring atmosphere.</p>\n</div>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div Wenkenhof</div>\n<span class=\"loc-overlay__personen is-handwritten\">Up to 200 people</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__description text-size-regular\"><p>The Wenkenhof Villa was built in 1735 as a representative summer residence in the French style. The ground floor features three magnificent Baroque rooms with stucco ceilings and elaborate wall decorations. The villa offers a unique ambience for stylish parties, aperitifs, and banquets. In summer, the large terrace overlooking the gardens expands the available space.</p>\n</div>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__info-heading\">CAPACITY</div>\n<div The spacious, adaptable hall is ideal for banquets, corporate events, exhibitions or cultural events. The combination of rustic charm and elegant atmosphere creates a unique setting for special occasions.</p>\n</div>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__info-heading\">CAPACITY</div>\n<div class=\"info-grid\">\n<div class=\"info-item\" style=\"grid-column: span 3;\">\n<span class=\"info-item__text\">Banquet<br>\nup to 300 people</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"info-item\" style=\"grid-column: span 3;\">\n<span class=\"info-item__text\">Standing aperitif<br>\nup to 400 people</span>\n</div>\n</div>\n</section>\n<section class=\"loc-overlay__info\">\n<div class=\"loc-overlay__info-heading\">AREA</div>\n<div class=\"info-grid\">\n<div class=\"info-item\" style=\"grid-column: span 6;\">\n<span });\n}\nfunction initGallery(scope) {\nif (typeof Splide === 'undefined') return;\nscope.querySelectorAll('.loc-overlay__gallery.marquee-slider').forEach(function (wrapper) {\nvar track = wrapper.querySelector('.marquee-track');\nif (!track || track.dataset.initialized) return;\ntrack.classList.add('splide');\nvar items = Array.prototype.slice.call(track.querySelectorAll('.marquee-item'));\nif (!items.length) return;\nvar splideTrack = document.createElement('div');\nsplideTrack.className = 'splide__track';\nvar list = document.createElement('ul');\nlist.className = 'splide__list';\nitems.forEach(function (item) {\nvar li = document.createElement('li');\nli.className = 'splide__slide';\nli.appendChild(item);\nlist.appendChild(li);\n});\nsplideTrack.appendChild(list);\ntrack.appendChild(splideTrack);\ntrack.dataset.initialized = 'true';\nvar ext = (window.splide && window.splide.Extensions) ? window.splide.Extensions : undefined;\nnew Splide(track, {\ntype: 'loop', drag: 'free', focus: 'center', perPage: 1,\nautoWidth: true, gap: '12px', arrows: false, pagination: false,\nautoScroll: { speed: 0.5, pauseOnHover: true, pauseOnFocus: false }\n}).mount(ext);\n});\n}\nfunction open(overlay, trigger) {\nif (closeTimer) { clearTimeout(closeTimer); closeTimer = null; }\nif (current && current !== overlay) { current.classList.remove('is-open'); current.hidden = true; }\ncurrent = overlay;\nlastTrigger = trigger || null;\noverlay.hidden = false;\nvar scroller = overlay.querySelector('.loc-overlay__inner');\nif (scroller) scroller.scrollTop = 0; last.focus(); }\nelse if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) { event.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }\n}\n});\n}\nfunction init() { initSliders(); initShowMore(); initOverlays(); }\nif (document.readyState === 'loading') {\ndocument.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n} else {\ninit();\n}\n})();\n</script>\n<script id=\"gamma-team-js\">\n(function () {\n'use strict';\nfunction shuffle(arr) {\nfor (var i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\nvar j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\nvar t = arr[i]; arr[i] = arr[j]; arr[j] = t;\n}\nreturn arr;\n}\n/* Slider (Home): shuffle → Splide, Desktop 4 sichtbar */\nfunction initSliders() {\nif (typeof Splide === 'undefined') {\nconsole.warn('[team] Splide nicht geladen - Slider uebersprungen');\nreturn;\n}\ndocument.querySelectorAll('[data-team-slider]').forEach(function (el) {\nif (el.dataset.initialized) return;\nel.dataset.initialized = 'true';\nvar list = el.querySelector('.splide__list');\nif (list) {\nshuffle(Array.prototype.slice.call(list.children)).forEach(function (s) { list.appendChild(s); });\n}\nvar splide = new Splide(el, {\ntype: 'slide', perPage: 4, perMove: 4, gap: 0,\nspeed: 600, easing: 'cubic-bezier(0.37, 0, 0.63, 1)',\narrows: false, pagination: false, drag: true,\nbreakpoints: {\n1119: { perPage: 3, perMove: 3 },\n767:  { perPage: 2, perMove: 2 },\n479:  { perPage: 1, perMove: 1 }\n}\n});\nsplide.mount();\nel.classList.add('is-active');\nvar section = el.closest('.locations-slider-section');\nvar prev = section ? section.querySelector('[data-team-prev]') : null;\nvar next = section ? section.querySelector('[data-team-next]') : null;\nvar wire = function (btn, dir) {\nif (!btn) return;\nif (!btn.hasAttribute('tabindex')) btn.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');\nvar go = function () { splide.go(dir); };\nbtn.addEventListener('click', go);\nbtn.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {\nif (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') { event.preventDefault(); go(); }\n});\n};\nwire(prev, '<');\nwire(next, '>');\nvar updateArrows = function () {\nvar end = splide.Components.Controller.getEnd();\nvar i = splide.index;\nif (prev) { prev.classList.toggle('is-disabled', i <= 0);  prev.setAttribute('aria-disabled', i <= 0 ? 'true' : 'false'); }\nif (next) { next.classList.toggle('is-disabled', i >= end); next.setAttribute('aria-disabled', i >= end ? 'true' : 'false'); }\n};\nsplide.on('mounted move updated', updateArrows);\nupdateArrows();\n});\n}\n/* Grid-Linien: liest die echte Reihen-Anordnung (offsetTop) → layout-agnostisch,\npasst sich automatisch an die Spaltenanzahl (4/3/2/1 je Breakpoint) an.\nRechte Border ausser am Reihenende; volle-Breite-Linie als eigenes Element\nzwischen den Reihen (ausser nach der letzten). */\nfunction computeGrid(grid) {\n// alte eingefuegte Linien entfernen, dann frisch messen\nArray.prototype.slice.call(grid.querySelectorAll('.team-grid__rowline')).forEach(function (l) {\nl.parentNode.removeChild(l);\n});\nvar items = Array.prototype.slice.call(grid.querySelectorAll('.team-item')).filter(function (it) {\nreturn !it.classList.contains('is-hidden') && it.offsetParent !== null;\n});\nitems.forEach(function (it) { it.classList.remove('is-row-end');
